### [17] [HiBRIDGE: A Hierarchical Bayesian Neural Network Framework for Interpretable Dialogue Management in Group-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08678)
*Massimiliano Nigro,Hatice Gunes,Micol Spitale,Fethiye Irmak Dogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiBRIDGE，一种用于多人群体人机对话管理的分层贝叶斯神经网络框架，在不确定性建模、有限数据学习、行为决策结构化及解释性方面优于多种基线，并通过离线和在线实验验证了其有效性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人交互中，机器人需要持续决定交流对象和对话行为，但多个行为往往同时合理，选择取决于参与者关系与上下文。现有方法在表达行为不确定性、利用有限交互数据，以及将决策分解为具有语义意义且便于解释的中间步骤方面存在不足。

Method: 提出HiBRIDGE分层贝叶斯神经网络框架，将行为选择建模为结构化的多阶段决策过程，并利用贝叶斯建模实现不确定性感知和小样本鲁棒学习。进一步训练决策树代理模型，用于分析分层决策结构是否能够生成更易理解的机器人行为解释。实验包括三个离线群体人机交互数据集、一个在线解释评价研究（N=20）和一个面对面实时交互研究（N=12）。

Result: 在三个离线数据集上，贝叶斯模型整体优于确定性模型及多种先进基线。在线研究显示，基于分层模型生成的解释在理解帮助性方面评分更高，也更受参与者偏好。面对面研究表明，HiBRIDGE能够支持自主实时群体交互，分层与扁平贝叶斯模型均获得积极评价。

Conclusion: HiBRIDGE有效结合了贝叶斯不确定性建模与分层决策结构，在预测性能、有限数据适应性和行为解释性之间取得平衡。研究结果表明，具有语义结构的分层决策不仅有助于机器人进行群体对话管理，也能提升用户对机器人行为的理解。

Abstract: In multi-party human-robot interaction, a robot must continuously decide whom to address and what to say to participate effectively in the conversation. In real-world interactions, this is challenging because several behaviours may be plausible at the same time: a robot might continue a topic with one participant, involve another through a question, or address the whole group, with the appropriate choice depending on both whom it addresses and the interaction context. Current approaches remain limited in representing uncertainty when several behaviours are plausible and in structuring decisions into semantically meaningful intermediate steps that make robot decisions easier to interpret. Addressing these, we present HiBRIDGE, a hierarchical Bayesian neural network framework for group-robot dialogue management. Its Bayesian formulation enables uncertainty-aware prediction and robust learning from limited interaction data, while the hierarchical approach formulates behaviour selection as a structured, multi-stage decision process. We further use decision-tree surrogates to investigate whether this structure can support more interpretable explanations. Across three offline group-HRI datasets, our findings show that Bayesian formulations outperform their deterministic counterparts and several state-of-the-art baselines. Next, through an online study (N=20), we show that explanations derived from the hierarchical model are rated as more helpful for understanding robot behaviour and are preferred over those derived from the flat model. Finally, through our in-person study (N=12), we demonstrate the feasibility of HiBRIDGE for autonomous real-time group interaction, with both hierarchical and flat Bayesian variants positively perceived. Overall, HiBRIDGE combines strong predictive performance with a structured decision process that supports more interpretable explanations of robot behaviour.

</details>


### [18] [Estimating Semantic Ambiguity via Gaussian Context Distributions for VLM-Driven Traversability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08583)
*Ramona Häuselmann,Mario A.V. Saucedo,Christoforos Kanellakis,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向非结构化环境自主导航的视觉可通行性估计方法，通过概念锚定将视觉语言模型的开放词汇预测映射到连续物理可通行性尺度，并利用高斯上下文分布同时生成可通行性图与不确定性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在复杂户外场景中容易产生语义歧义，尤其是在视觉伪影、混合地形和上下文冲突存在时，单一预测结果难以反映其可靠性。为提升自主导航的安全性与鲁棒性，需要显式建模预测不确定性。

Method: 方法首先采用概念锚定，将视觉语言模型输出的开放词汇概念 grounding 到连续的物理可通行性尺度；随后将模型响应建模为高斯上下文分布，并依据该分布的统计特性生成稠密可通行性地图和稠密不确定性地图。

Result: 在真实世界 GOOSE 数据集上的实验表明，所提出的不确定性指标能够有效对应视觉伪影、混合地形重叠等语义歧义来源。该方法在性能上具有竞争力，同时能够提供统计意义上的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该研究将视觉语言模型的语义预测与连续可通行性估计及不确定性建模相结合，为复杂户外环境中的安全自主导航提供了更可靠的感知基础。其主要贡献在于不仅预测地形是否可通行，还能量化预测的可信程度。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in unstructured environments requires robust scene understanding, yet Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often suffer from semantic ambiguity, where conflicting predictions can lead to dangerous failures. To address this, we present a novel pipeline for vision-based traversability estimation that explicitly models contextual uncertainty. Our approach utilizes Conceptual Anchoring to ground open-vocabulary VLM predictions onto a continuous physical traversability scale. By formulating the model's responses as a Gaussian Context Distribution (GCD), we derive both a dense traversability map and a dense uncertainty map based on the statistical properties of the distribution. Experimental validation on the real-world GOOSE dataset demonstrates that our proposed uncertainty metric effectively correlates with sources of ambiguity, such as visual artifacts and mixed terrain overlap. The method exhibits competitive performance while offering the distinct advantage of providing statistical uncertainty estimates to address semantic ambiguity, enabling safer and more reliable autonomous behavior in complex outdoor settings.

</details>


### [19] [TASG-Explore: Traversability-Aware Sector-Guided Exploration for Ground Robot on Uneven Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08512)
*Shaocong Wang,Shiliang Shao,Ting Wang,Guangjie Han,Lianqing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TASG-Explore，一种面向非均匀地形的可通行性分析与扇区引导探索框架，在探索效率、覆盖完整性和地形安全之间实现平衡，并在多种复杂环境中取得优于六种代表性方法的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非均匀地形中的自主探索存在效率与可靠性之间的矛盾：精细地形推理能够提高局部规划安全性，但计算开销较大；粗粒度区域引导虽然适合快速扩展，却容易遗漏狭窄通道和不规则可通行边界。因此，需要一种兼顾多尺度地形理解、探索覆盖和规划效率的方法。

Method: 框架首先通过可变体素地面拟合和自适应8位障碍物编码开展分层可通行性分析；随后将代价地图划分为多个扇区，增量更新扇区聚类，提取与地形耦合的前沿观测视点，并维护包含未知拓扑假设的动态拓扑路线图；最后利用扇区引导规划器选择区域目标，并插入局部视点生成探索路径。

Result: 在洞穴、森林和崎岖山地等多种复杂环境的基准实验中，TASG-Explore在六种代表性先进规划器中取得最佳综合表现。可通行性分析在保持较高精度的同时，将处理效率提升6.3倍；探索规划效率提升51%，并在崎岖山地场景中将覆盖率提高最多2.95倍。大规模真实环境实验进一步验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: TASG-Explore通过分层可通行性建模、扇区级区域引导与局部视点规划，有效融合了全局探索效率和局部地形安全性。实验结果表明，该方法适合复杂非结构化环境中的地面机器人自主探索，但摘要尚未说明其对传感器噪声、动态障碍物和极端地形参数变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration on uneven terrain requires ground robots to balance exploration efficiency, coverage completeness, and terrain safety. Detailed tsrrain reasoning improves local reliability but can slow large-scale exploration, whereas coarse region guidance expands quickly in open areas but can miss narrow passages and irregular traversable boundaries. To address this challenge, this paper presents TASG-Explore, a traversability-aware sector-guided exploration framework for ground robots. The framework first performs hierarchical traversability analysis using variable-voxel ground fitting and adaptive 8-bit obstacle encoding. It then splitting cost map into sectors, incrementally updates sector clusters, extracts terrain-coupled frontier viewpoints, and maintains a dynamic topological roadmap with unknown topological hypotheses. Finally, a sector-guided planner selects region targets and inserts local viewpoints to generate efficient exploration routes. Benchmark experiments in diverse challenging environments, including caves, forests, and rugged hills, show that TASG-Explore achieves the best overall performance among six representative state-of-the-art planners. The proposed traversability analysis improves processing efficiency by 6.3 times while maintaining high accuracy, and the exploration planner improves exploration efficiency by 51% and increases coverage by up to 2.95 times in rugged hill scene. Large-scale real-world experiments further demonstrate the practical value of the proposed method.

</details>


### [20] [PGMT: Perceptive General Motion Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08511)
*Hongyi Li,Li Peizhuo,Yucheng Tao,Ze Wang,Fangzhou Xu,Jinyi Chen,Yanyan Yuan,Dapeng Jia,Yongbin Jin,Mingfeng Fan,Guillaume Sartoretti,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PGMT（感知式通用运动跟踪）框架，使人形机器人能够在复杂地形上自适应执行多样化全身动作，并在Unitree G1上实现零样本真实环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人运动跟踪依赖与地形无关的参考动作，在崎岖或有障碍地形上容易产生物理不可行的动作，导致跟踪性能下降。因此，需要在保留原始动作意图的同时，根据地形进行必要调整。

Method: PGMT分阶段学习：首先构建通用运动跟踪与恢复先验；随后引入由动作条件驱动的地形感知机制，仅编码与当前动作相关的地形区域；同时采用地形感知的跟踪松弛策略，允许机器人偏离参考轨迹以适应环境，但保持动作的核心意图。

Result: 在Unitree G1上的零样本实验表明，该方法能够在包含障碍物的真实复杂地形中实现稳健的地形自适应行走和全身动作执行，跨越最高约37厘米的障碍，并支持远程操控、动态动作跟踪和跌倒恢复。

Conclusion: PGMT将通用人形运动跟踪扩展到非平坦复杂环境，形成了一个统一策略，可同时支持地形自适应移动、多样化全身行为、远程操控与恢复能力，显示出较好的泛化性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Humanoid motion trackers can reproduce diverse whole-body motions, but their performance degrades on complex terrain where terrain-agnostic references become physically infeasible. We present PGMT, a Perceptive General Motion Tracking pipeline for humanoid robots that learns terrain adaptation from independently selected motion references and terrains. PGMT first learns a general tracking and recovery prior, then incorporates terrain perception through motion-conditioned terrain glimpses that selectively encode regions relevant to the current motion. Terrain-aware tracking relaxation allows necessary deviations from the reference while preserving its motion intent. Zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 demonstrates robust terrain-adaptive locomotion and whole-body motion execution over real-world terrain with obstacles up to 37 cm high, while supporting teleoperation, dynamic motion tracking, and fall recovery. PGMT extends general humanoid motion tracking beyond flat ground, providing a unified policy for terrain-adaptive locomotion, diverse whole-body behaviors, and teleoperation in complex environments.

</details>


### [21] [Multi-bounce Drum Roll with Optimized Active Tricks to Leverage Soft Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08490)
*Naoto Yamanaka,Takanori Jin,Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软体机器人鼓手，通过Tap-Pull（TP）和Micro-Pulse（MP）两种微动作，并结合贝叶斯优化，实现高频、稳定且高效的鼓点连击。软体TP每次击打平均产生12.25次反弹，软体MP的声学效率达到刚性MP的6.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频鼓点连击依赖鼓槌的一次击打产生多次反弹。现有机器人演奏不仅需要利用软体结构的弹性，还需要精确控制击打过程中的细微运动，以增加反弹次数并减缓音量衰减。

Method: 设计两种动作策略：TP在击打后加入回拉动作，以增加反弹次数；MP在击打过程中注入微小振荡，以维持演奏音量。针对软体系统的非线性和复杂性，采用数据驱动的贝叶斯优化分别调节两种策略，并在软体和刚性末端执行器上进行实验比较。

Result: 优化后的软体TP实现了每次击打12.25次反弹，并保持较均匀的反弹间隔；软体MP有效抑制了音量衰减，其声学效率达到刚性MP的6.8倍。

Conclusion: 软体结构的弹性与精细动作控制相结合，并通过贝叶斯优化进行参数调节，能够显著提升机器人鼓点连击的反弹次数、节奏稳定性和声学效率，验证了该方法用于高质量鼓点演奏的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a soft robotic drummer for accurate and efficient drum rolls. High-frequency drum rolls require the "multi-bounce technique," where a drumstick bounces multiple times with a single stroke. In robotic reproduction of this technique, the body's elasticity is key, while the fine motion during the stroke is also crucial for maximizing the potential of that elasticity. Therefore, we design two tricks: i) Tap-Pull (TP) trick to increase the number of rebounds by adding a pulling motion after impact; and ii) Micro-Pulse (MP) trick to keep the drumming volume by injecting small oscillations during the stroke. Due to the nonlinear complexity of soft embodiment, both tricks are efficiently tuned using Bayesian optimization in a data-driven manner for accomplishing the respective objectives quantified. We evaluated the optimized behaviors with soft and rigid end-effectors. As a result, the soft TP achieved the highest bounce count (12.25 per stroke) with uniform intervals. The soft MP suppressed the volume decay, yielding 6.8-times higher acoustic efficiency compared to the rigid MP. These results indicate that the proposed tricks with the combination of elasticity and optimization can make robots play excellent drum rolls.

</details>


### [22] [Safe Task Planning with Long-Term Graph Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08444)
*Siyuan Li,Taiyan Lang,Aoqi Yan,Jia Yu,Feifan Liu,Yihan Du,Yu Zheng,Xun Wang,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeMem是一种面向具身智能体的安全任务规划框架，通过构建长期语义图记忆，并结合基于大语言模型的风险预测器，在部分可观测环境中评估候选动作、识别潜在危险并触发保守性可调的重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型或视觉语言模型的零样本任务规划方法缺乏物理风险意识，尤其难以处理视野之外的潜在障碍与危险，导致高层动作可能不安全。

Method: SafeMem根据智能体的第一视角观测，增量式记录环境中的物体及其关系，构建并维护开放动态环境的长期语义图记忆。随后，基于大语言模型的风险预测器利用图记忆评估候选动作；当检测到风险时，系统结合风险解释启动保守性可调的重规划闭环。

Result: 在IS-Bench基准和真实机器人平台上的大量实验表明，相较于现有先进的视觉语言模型任务规划器，SafeMem显著提升了安全成功率。

Conclusion: 长期语义记忆能够弥补部分可观测条件下的环境信息缺失，结合风险预测与解释驱动的重规划，可有效增强具身智能体任务规划的安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly advanced zero-shot task planning for embodied agents. However, most LLM- and VLM-driven methods struggle to generate safe high-level actions due to a lack of physical risk awareness, particularly under partial observability, where hazards lie outside the immediate field of view. To address this challenge, we propose a novel safe task-planning framework, SafeMem, which constructs and maintains a long-term semantic graph memory of the open and dynamic environment. Based on egocentric observations, the proposed framework incrementally accumulates knowledge about surrounding objects and their relationships with a graph. Then, an LLM-based risk predictor evaluates candidate actions using the graph memory, triggering a conservatism-modulated replanning loop with explanations for detected hazards. Extensive experiments on the IS-Bench benchmark and a real-world robot platform demonstrate that the SafeMem framework substantially improves safe success rates compared to state-of-the-art VLM-driven task planners. Video results are available on our webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [Coverage Path Planning for Redundant Manipulators using Generalized Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08409)
*Raksi Kopo,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将经典的表面覆盖生成树方法扩展至任务冗余机械臂，提出离线与在线关节生成树覆盖算法，以优化逆运动学构型选择和覆盖路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面覆盖任务中，同一网格点通常对应多个逆运动学解。不同构型会显著影响关节运动量、重构次数、路径连续性及规划效率，因此需要在保证覆盖完整性的同时合理选择构型。

Method: 离线JSTC在每个网格单元采样多个逆运动学解，将构型选择建模为广义最小生成树问题，为每个单元选定一个构型并遍历生成覆盖路径。在线JSTC则通过增量扩展和回溯生成树，结合可行性与代价评估处理动态网格更新。

Result: 仿真结果表明，离线JSTC相较其他方法可减少计算时间、构型重构次数和关节运动量；在线JSTC在动态场景中具有较快的单步规划速度，并能够适应环境更新。

Conclusion: JSTC为冗余机械臂表面覆盖提供了统一的构型选择与路径规划框架。离线方法适合追求整体运动质量的静态任务，在线方法适合需要快速响应的动态覆盖任务。

Abstract: Surface coverage with task-redundant manipulators is challenging because each surface point may admit multiple inverse kinematics (IK) solutions, and configuration choices strongly affect motion quality. This paper extends the classical Spanning Tree Coverage (STC) method to redundant manipulators through offline and online Joint Spanning Tree Coverage (JSTC) algorithms. Offline JSTC samples multiple Inverse Kinematics (IK) solutions per grid cell and formulates the problem as a Generalized Minimum Spanning Tree (GMST), selecting one configuration per cell and tracing the resulting tree to obtain a non-revisiting coverage path. Online JSTC incrementally expands and backtracks a spanning tree with feasibility and cost evaluation while handling dynamic grid updates. Simulation results show that offline JSTC reduces computation time, reconfigurations, and joint motion compared to other methods, while online JSTC achieves fast per-step planning in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [24] [Localized Visual Feature Aggregation via Focus Pooling for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08408)
*Ruiyu Wang,Zheyu Zhuang,Danica Kragic,Florian T. Pokorny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FocusPool，一种基于机器人本体状态引导的注意力池化模块，用于从CNN中间特征中选择并聚合与控制相关的局部视觉信息，从而提升视觉运动策略的数据效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略通常通过裁剪RGB图像或点云来显式聚焦控制相关区域，但这类输入预处理增加了系统设计成本。尽管CNN中间特征保留了局部视觉上下文，传统池化方法难以有效提取和聚合这些信息，因此需要一种直接作用于编码特征的局部信息聚合机制。

Method: FocusPool以机器人的当前本体感知状态作为条件，利用注意力机制在CNN中间特征的空间位置上进行选择性加权与聚合，形成包含任务进程相关及控制相关局部信息的视觉表示，并将该表示直接用于策略学习。训练时仅更新编码器5.8%的参数。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，FocusPool相较于常规池化方法和显式局部聚焦方法，策略成功率分别提升36.2%和41.2%，表明其能够更有效地提取控制所需的局部视觉信息。

Conclusion: CNN中间特征确实包含适用于控制的局部视觉上下文，但需要任务条件化的注意力聚合才能充分利用。FocusPool在较少参数训练成本下提升了视觉运动策略的性能和数据效率，为从通用视觉编码器中提取控制相关信息提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Focusing on spatially localized, control-relevant visual cues has been shown to improve data efficiency in visuomotor policies by reducing the need to model task-irrelevant visual variation. Existing methods often impose this focus through input preprocessing, such as cropping control- or object-centric regions in RGB images or point-clouds. However, it remains underexplored whether such localized features can be exposed directly from commonly used convolutional neural network (CNN) encoded features. In this paper, we show that intermediate CNN features preserve localized visual context for control, but existing pooling methods fail to aggregate it effectively. We introduce FocusPool, an attention pooling module that selectively aggregates intermediate visual features according to their relevance to the robot's current proprioceptive context. The resulting pooled representation captures task-progressive, control-relevant local information and is used directly for policy learning. Across simulation and real-world experiments, FocusPool improves policy success rates over pooling and explicit local focus methods by 36.2% and 41.2%, with training only 5.8% of encoder parameters.

</details>


### [25] [EvoNav-Bench: Benchmarking Lifelong Navigation in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08292)
*Xilin Wang,Guoxi Zhang,Hongming Xu,Zhuofan Zhang,Tianxu Wang,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoNav-Bench，用于评估长期导航智能体在环境持续变化时复用历史场景信息的能力。实验表明，现有方法对环境演化较为脆弱，而简单的更新与重置策略可用于分析并缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期导航需要智能体在同一环境中连续完成多个子任务，并复用此前积累的场景表示以减少重复探索。然而，现有方法通常假设环境静态不变，可能将过时观测与新观测错误融合；现有基准也无法有效暴露这一失效模式。因此，本文旨在建立能够系统评估环境变化影响的长期导航基准。

Method: 基于ProcTHOR框架，构建EvoNav-Bench，并在类似GOAT-Bench的长期导航设定中，于不同导航任务之间引入受控的环境修改，使历史经验仍然有用但不再完全可靠。作者评测了三种依赖场景表示构建与复用的近期方法，并比较了三种处理环境变化的启发式策略：Frontier-Update、Fail-then-Update和Stage-Reset。

Result: 实验显示，现有场景表示方法在环境演化条件下表现出明显脆弱性，复用过时观测会影响导航性能。三种启发式策略能够支持对环境变化影响的受控分析，并在一定程度上减轻其负面影响。

Conclusion: EvoNav-Bench填补了动态环境长期导航评测的空缺，揭示了静态环境假设下方法的泛化局限。未来的长期导航系统需要具备环境变化检测、历史信息更新以及不确定性管理能力，而不能直接依赖未经验证的旧场景表示。

Abstract: Lifelong navigation (LN) requires an embodied agent to solve a sequence of navigation subtasks in the same environment. Since solving each subtask from scratch incurs redundant exploration, an LN agent must consolidate experience from earlier stages and reuse it in later stages, often through persistent scene representations such as scene graphs or visual snapshots. However, existing approaches typically assume a stationary environment, whereas in real-world LN settings, human activities can cause the environment to evolve. With the stationary assumption violated, existing methods may fuse outdated prior observations with new observations, yet current benchmarks cannot reveal this failure mode. In this paper, we present EvoNav-Bench, which extends the GOAT-Bench style LN formulation in the context of evolving environments. Built on the ProcTHOR framework, EvoNav-Bench introduces environment modifications between navigation tasks, making prior experience useful but not fully reliable. This design enables controlled evaluation of how environment evolution affects LN agents that reuse prior scene observations. Using EvoNav-Bench, we benchmark three recent methods that build and reuse scene representations for navigation. We also compare three simple heuristic strategies for handling environment evolution: Frontier-Update, Fail-then-Update, and Stage-Reset. Our results show that existing methods are brittle under environment evolution, while the heuristic strategies enable a controlled analysis of how agents can adapt to scene changes and mitigate their impact.

</details>


### [26] [Seeing is Not Believing: Breaking the Physical-to-Digital Trust Boundary in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08280)
*Leming Shen,Shikai Geng,Yuanqing Zheng,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示了ROS 2远程验证链路中的严重信任漏洞：攻击者仅需修改一个环境变量，即可通过预构建钩子拦截和注入遥测数据及控制信号，从而操纵机器人行为并伪造验证信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作依赖下游验证器通过远程证明检查机器人是否按任务执行，但现有机制默认传感器遥测可信。论文旨在评估这一信任假设及第三方容器和辅助工具带来的供应链风险。

Method: 研究人员分析ROS 2的数据与控制流程，设计基于环境变量触发的恶意钩子，在数据发布前实时截获、篡改并注入遥测和控制信号；同时评估通过受污染的Docker容器及软件包传播攻击的可行性，并在配备Secure ROS 2的Franka Emika机械臂上进行实体验证。

Result: 攻击能够在不破坏硬件的情况下实时注入伪造遥测，抖动约为3毫秒，保持了时间同步；即使面对基于人工智能的检测器，攻击成功率仍达到87%。研究还表明，第三方容器和辅助工具会显著降低攻击部署门槛。

Conclusion: ROS 2中的遥测与控制链路不能被默认视为可信，单一环境变量配置和供应链依赖即可形成高影响攻击面。系统需要加强钩子加载控制、运行时完整性验证、容器及依赖包审计，并采用独立且不可被同一路径篡改的传感与验证机制。论文已向ROS 2开发团队负责任地披露相关问题。

Abstract: In multi-robot collaboration, task handovers rely on downstream verifiers performing remote attestation, which inspects sensor telemetry to ensure a robot's physical behavior strictly matches its assigned task. But can this telemetry be trusted? We show that it often cannot. In this paper, we uncover a severe vulnerability in Robot Operating System (ROS) 2: by modifying a single environment variable, an adversary can execute a pre-built hook to covertly intercept and inject both telemetry and control signals before they are published. Consequently, adversaries can hijack a robot to perform dangerous tasks while spoofing downstream verifiers with synthesized fake telemetry. Worse still, by exploiting the widespread reliance on third-party Docker containers and auxiliary tools, attackers can distribute compromised packages embedded with these malicious hooks to launch such attacks easily. On a physical Franka Emika robotic arm running Secure ROS 2, our attack injects fabricated telemetry in real time with only around 3 ms of jitter, preserving temporal synchronization and hardware integrity while achieving an 87% success rate even against an AI-based detector. We have responsibly disclosed these findings to the ROS 2 development team. We prepared a demo video available at this https URL .

</details>


### [27] [Bridging Language and Physics: Automated Design of Continuum Robots with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08220)
*Jingyi Chen,Mohan Zhang,Laura Yao,Yingtai Ni,Jianmin Ji,Jie Peng,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AID-SR多层框架，通过将仿真器观测到的物理状态转化为结构化反馈，结合语义批评、人类反馈和迭代优化，引导大语言模型自动设计物理可行、功能有效的腱驱动连续体机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型能够依据高层规格生成机器人设计，但难以推理具体形态与复杂物理交互之间的关系，导致设计方案物理有效性较低。研究旨在缩小语言推理与实体具身效果之间的差距。

Method: 构建AID-SR闭环设计框架，将机器人设计、仿真评估、物理状态反馈和大语言模型迭代优化连接起来，并融合语义批评与人类反馈。研究在覆盖到达、抓取、移动和操作等任务的14项基准上评估腱驱动连续体机器人设计，并使用统一的强化学习流程进行任务训练，进一步制造部分设计开展真实环境验证。

Result: 框架生成的设计有96.2%通过仿真可行性检查；经过统一强化学习训练后，26.7%的机器人能够成功完成对应任务。研究还制造了3个由AID-SR设计的机器人，并在真实环境中成功完成任务。

Conclusion: AID-SR通过仿真驱动的结构化反馈和多轮迭代，有效提升了大语言模型自动设计连续体机器人的物理可行性与任务功能性。仿真和真实实验结果表明，该方法能够推动大语言模型用于复杂机器人设计，并具备一定的现实落地能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a promising tool for automating robot design from high-level specifications, yet they remain ineffective for robots operating under complex physical interactions. This limitation stems from the gap between language-based reasoning and the physical consequences of embodiment, often resulting in designs with low physical validity. In this work, we propose a multi-layered framework, AID-SR, that establishes a closed loop by translating simulator-observed physical states into structured feedback for the LLM designer. Combined with semantic critique, human feedback, and iterative refinement, the framework promotes the generation of physically feasible and functionally meaningful robot designs. We evaluate our approach on tendon-driven continuum robots across a benchmark of 14 tasks spanning reaching, grasping, locomotion, and manipulation. The proposed framework achieves 96.2% rate for passing the simulation feasibility check and by applying a common reinforcement learning training, 26.7% robots can successfully fulfill the corresponding task. We then fabricate three designed robots of AID-SR that successfully complete the task in real-world. These extensive experiments across simulation and real-world environments demonstrate and break the wall of utilizing the LLMs for automated design of continuum robots. The source code and experimental resources are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [28] [TacClip: a clip-on sensor measures dynamic contact forces without covering the fingerpads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08214)
*Yuqian Ye,Hao Li,Jingxi Xu,Haojun Feng,Seongheon Hong,Mark R. Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacClip是一种安装在指甲上的无电子可穿戴装置，利用光纤布拉格光栅（FBG）记录指尖因接触力和振动产生的形变，在保留指腹触觉的同时实现动态接触感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部追踪或触觉采集装置常遮挡指腹、影响用户对纹理、摩擦、温度和细微表面特征的自然感知。TacClip旨在以低负担方式测量接触力与探索性滑动中的动态振动，并兼容视觉或手套式手部追踪系统。

Method: 研究者将FBG嵌入小型塑料夹，并将其安装在指甲上，通过指尖形变推断接触力和振动。针对视觉追踪场景，可在聚酰亚胺条带上增加FBG以辅助姿态估计。装置不含电子元件，因此可在水下使用，同时保持指腹裸露接触。

Result: 在0至8 N的手指按压测试中，TacClip估计力大小的典型误差低于0.5 N。在布料操作和胶带边缘寻找实验中，装置能够捕捉探索性滑动产生的振动及动态接触事件，并支持水下测量。

Conclusion: TacClip以结构简单、低遮挡和无电子元件为特点，为指尖接触力及动态振动感知提供了一种有效方案。其兼容多种手部追踪系统，且不显著损害自然触觉，适合用于触觉交互、材料探索和水下操作研究。

Abstract: TacClip is a minimally encumbering wearable device for recording fingertip deformation caused by contact forces and vibrations. It can be combined with vision- or glove-based hand tracking systems that leave the fingertips uncovered and provides a measure of dynamic contact interactions, while leaving the finger pads exposed so that the user retains natural sensitivity to texture, friction, temperature, and fine surface features. The signal is produced by a Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) embedded on a small plastic clip mounted over the fingernail. Optionally, for use with vision-based tracking, additional FBGs on polyimide strips can complement camera-based pose estimation. In finger pressing tests, TacClip estimates the force magnitude with typical errors below $0.5~\mathrm{N}$ over a $0$--$8~\mathrm{N}$ range. In tests of cloth handling and tape edge finding, we show that it captures the vibrations and dynamic events generated during exploratory sliding. With no electronics, TacClip can also be used submerged in water, while preserving bare finger contact.

</details>


### [29] [Monkey See, Can Monkey Do? A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Skill Learning by Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08209)
*Weiwei Gu,Anmol Gupta,Anant Sah,Ryan Varghese,Lalitha Shreya Vanam,Prabhath Adireddi,Peter Karkus,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboReel是一个统一的机器人从人类视频中学习（LfO）基准，用于在十项操作任务上系统评估不同模型、表示方式与算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfO研究在假设、硬件和环境设置上差异较大，导致方法之间难以公平比较，也难以准确判断领域进展。机器人操作数据获取成本高，因此需要可扩展且标准化的评测框架。

Method: 构建包含真实人类示范视频、模拟机器人轨迹和评测环境的基准，覆盖十项操作任务。设计四类测试套件，从多个维度评估模型，包括视觉干扰鲁棒性和长时序任务完成能力；同时比较不同表示方式以及七种以上先进LfO算法，并纳入基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的变体。

Result: RoboReel实现了跨模型、表示和任务设置的统一评测。分析表明，当前方法在长时序任务和容错范围较低的任务上仍面临明显困难，视觉干扰等因素也能够揭示模型鲁棒性的差异。

Conclusion: RoboReel为从人类视频学习机器人策略提供了标准化、可复现的比较平台，并指出了当前研究的关键瓶颈，尤其是长时序规划、精确控制和复杂视觉条件下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning from Observation (LfO) is a fundamental robotic capability that replicates how humans and animals socially learn from each other. Beyond its biological parallels, this modality provides a practical solution for data scaling in sample-inefficient and data-starved domains like robotics. Recent work has demonstrated promising results in learning manipulation skills from human videos, yet progress in this area remains difficult to assess. Existing methods vary widely in assumptions, hardware choices, and environment setups making it difficult to draw meaningful comparisons and identify advances in the field. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboReel: a unified benchmark for evaluating models that learn policies from human videos. RoboReel consists of bundled real-world human demonstration videos, simulated robot trajectories, and evaluation environments on ten manipulation tasks. We develop four test suites to evaluate the models' performance on multiple axes, including the robustness to visual distractors and the ability to complete long-horizon tasks. Our benchmark covers learning-from-observation models from different categories, and studies the effectiveness of multiple representation choices in our benchmark evaluation that covers over seven state-of-the-art algorithms (including our VLA based variants) in the field of LfO. Finally, we present an analysis of the different types of algorithms showing that long-horizon tasks and tasks with low tolerances are still challenging for current models. Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [30] [OmniNav: Robust Long-Horizon Target Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08159)
*Yujie Tang,Meiling Wang,Jinhao Jiang,Sibo Zuo,Yinan Deng,Xinyu Zhang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniNav将长时程目标导航建模为对场景有效性、目标位置信念和交互可行性的持续联合推断，并通过可更新场景记忆、证据感知的贝叶斯信念修正及面向操作的终点选择，提高语义ObjectNav与实例导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程导航面临部分可观测和动态环境带来的三类问题：场景信息会因物体移动或消失而过时，搜索失败应改变目标位置判断，而几何上可达的导航终点未必满足后续抓取或操作约束。现有方法通常缺乏对这些状态不确定性及其相互影响的统一处理。

Method: OmniNav将任务状态表示为因子化后验，联合建模场景有效性、目标信念和交互可行性。系统增量构建可更新的三维物体场景记忆，抑制过时证据传播；利用依赖感知的语义区域先验和负证据，对目标位置进行证据感知的贝叶斯更新，并据此选择前沿区域；在导航终点选择中显式加入操作可达性与碰撞约束，同时通过分层闭环恢复机制利用执行反馈修正后续决策。

Result: 在语义ObjectNav和细粒度实例导航基准上，OmniNav取得对比方法中的最高成功率，并对目标重新定位保持较强鲁棒性。在真实世界抓取放置任务中，相较改造后的开环基线，成功率由53.3%提升至71.7%，绝对提升18.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将场景记忆更新、目标信念修正和操作约束统一纳入持续闭环推断，能够有效提升长时程导航的可靠性与实际操作成功率。摘要中的主要贡献具有清晰的问题对应关系，但尚未给出各模块的独立消融结果、计算开销及跨环境泛化细节。

Abstract: Long-horizon target navigation requires a robot to sustain task execution across evolving observations, decisions, and physical interactions. This requires three coupled capabilities: maintaining valid scene memory, revising target beliefs under partial observability, and selecting interaction-feasible navigation endpoints. However, the state underlying each capability is only conditionally valid: scene representations become stale when objects move or disappear, unsuccessful searches alter beliefs over target locations, and geometrically convenient endpoints may still be infeasible for manipulation. To address these challenges, we present OmniNav, which formulates long-horizon navigation as continual inference over a factorized task state posterior coupling scene validity, target belief, and interaction feasibility. For representation, OmniNav incrementally constructs an updatable 3D object scene memory, preventing stale scene evidence from propagating to subsequent decisions. For exploration, it introduces an evidence-aware Bayesian belief-revision mechanism that derives dependency-aware region priors from semantic context, incorporates unsuccessful searches as negative evidence, and updates them for posterior-guided frontier selection. For interaction, OmniNav incorporates manipulation reachability and collision constraints into navigation-endpoint selection and propagates execution feedback through hierarchical closed-loop recovery. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniNav achieves the highest success rates among the compared methods on semantic ObjectNav and fine-grained instance navigation benchmarks, remains robust to target relocation, and improves real-world pick-and-place success from 53.3% to 71.7% over an adapted open-loop baseline. The project page of OmniNav is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [31] [mjorbit: A Simulation Framework for Space Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08010)
*John Z. Zhang,Joris Verhagen,Fausto Vega,Patrick McKeen,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出mjorbit，一种基于MuJoCo的多体空间机器人接触仿真通用框架，支持高效的大规模仿真，并面向在轨服务、装配与制造任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真框架难以高效融合轨道传播、航天器动力学及接触建模，限制了空间机器人在模型预测控制和强化学习中的应用。因此，本文旨在构建兼具通用性、性能和易用性的空间机器人仿真平台。

Method: 首先对轨道传播与机器人仿真框架的耦合方法开展经验性权衡研究；随后基于MuJoCo扩展实现mjorbit，加入航天器动力学、执行器和传感器，并提供低延迟C++ CPU后端、高吞吐GPU后端及简洁的Python接口。

Result: 该框架通过多个真实感在轨案例进行验证，覆盖模型预测控制和强化学习任务。实验表明，mjorbit能够支持多体空间机器人接触仿真，并兼顾计算效率、灵活性和大规模应用能力。

Conclusion: mjorbit为在轨服务、装配和制造提供了统一且高性能的空间机器人仿真基础设施，可用于控制算法与学习算法开发。作者同时开放了代码和示例，以促进复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a general framework for simulating multi-body space robots with contact. We bring efficient, large-scale robot simulation to in-space servicing, assembly, and manufacturing applications. First, we perform an empirical trade study of methods for coupling orbit propagation with existing robotics simulation frameworks. Next, we present mjorbit, a general, flexible, and performant framework built on the MuJoCo engine widely used in robotics, to which we add key spacecraft dynamics, actuators, and sensors. We provide a low-latency C++ CPU backend and a high-throughput GPU backend behind a simple Python API. We demonstrate mjorbit by solving several realistic on-orbit case studies with both model-predictive control and reinforcement learning. Open-source code and examples are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [32] [SPOT: Spatial Perception-Oriented Long-Horizon Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07933)
*Lixing Fang,Ziyan Xiong,Sunli Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPOT是一种面向空间感知的VR人形机器人遥操作系统，通过宽视场、稳定且可主动探索的机器人视角，提升长时程人形机器人示范数据采集能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量示范数据是通用人形机器人训练的瓶颈。现有遥操作系统存在视野狭窄、机器人运动导致相机视角不稳定，以及头部观察与机器人动作耦合等问题，限制了操作员对物体、环境和机器人姿态的持续空间感知，从而影响长时程移动操作任务。

Method: SPOT结合机器人搭载的双目鱼眼相机、宽视场立体显示、与机器人动作解耦的自由观察机制和视觉稳定技术，构建宽广、稳定且可主动检查的机器人中心视角。系统将机器人第一视角立体画面渲染到操作员周围的虚拟半球上，使操作员通过自然转头改变观察方向，而不会直接控制机器人头部、相机或躯干。

Result: 系统在跌落恢复、周边物体取回、大工作空间双手操作、精细对准和动态交互等依赖感知的任务上进行了评估。结果表明，SPOT提升了数据采集效率、操作准确性和恢复速度。

Conclusion: 通过扩展操作员的感知范围并解耦视觉探索与机器人执行，SPOT有效支持了长时程人形机器人移动操作示范数据的采集，具有良好的易用性和规模化应用潜力。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data is becoming a central bottleneck for training general-purpose humanoid robots. While recent humanoid teleoperation systems have made substantial progress in retargeting human motion to robot motion, long-horizon loco-manipulation requires another capability: operators must maintain task-relevant spatial awareness over time, e.g., object locations, surrounding environments, the robot's pose. We call the extent of this awareness the operator's perceptual horizon. However, existing methods often shorten this: narrow views miss peripheral events, robot-mounted cameras become unstable during locomotion, and coupled head-view control makes looking around interfere with robot motion. We present SPOT, a Spatial Perception-Oriented VR Teleoperation system for collecting long-horizon humanoid demonstration data by providing extended perceptual horizon. SPOT combines a robot-mounted binocular fisheye camera, a wide-field stereoscopic display, viewpoint-decoupled free-looking, and visual stabilization to provide a robot-centric view that is wide, stable, and actively inspectable. Unlike conventional egocentric interfaces, SPOT decouples visual exploration from robot actuation: the egocentric stereo observation is rendered on a virtual hemisphere around the operator, so natural head rotations change where the operator looks within the wide-field view rather than commanding the robot head, camera, or torso. We evaluate SPOT on perception-critical humanoid data-collection tasks spanning drop recovery, peripheral retrieval, large-workspace bimanual manipulation, fine alignment, and dynamic interaction. SPOT improves efficiency, accuracy, and recovery speed, demonstrating its effectiveness for user-friendly and scalable long-horizon humanoid data collection.

</details>


### [33] [A Multimodal Label Forecasting Method for Aperiodic Visuo-Motor Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07930)
*Borui He,Garrett E Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无周期性时间序列预测任务：基于第一视角视觉与本体感觉信息，预测类人机器人运动过程中的跌倒风险。作者构建仿真与真实硬件两个基准数据集，并提出融合内生与外生变量的新架构及严格独立同分布采样的训练流程，在真实数据和仿真数据上分别较现有方法提升至少12.73%和10.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测基准和方法通常隐含近似周期性假设，难以反映轨迹高度多样、周期性失效的真实场景。类人机器人运动中的跌倒预判为检验非周期性时间序列预测能力提供了新的应用任务和评测环境。

Method: 作者发布两个新基准数据集，分别来自仿真环境和真实硬件平台，用于评估无周期性条件下的时间序列预测。方法上提出融合内生变量与外生变量的深度学习架构，并设计严格保证训练样本独立同分布的训练流程，以减少数据相关性和采样偏差对模型评估的影响。

Result: 实验表明，现有Transformer类和MLP类时间序列预测方法在新基准上表现受限。所提方法在多种实验条件下均取得统计显著改进：真实数据上的提升至少为12.73%，仿真数据上的提升至少为10.40%。

Conclusion: 本文指出，周期性假设并不适用于所有时间序列预测场景，并通过机器人跌倒预判任务建立了面向非周期性预测的评测基准。融合多源变量并采用严格独立同分布采样的模型与训练策略能够有效提升预测性能，为非周期性时间序列预测研究提供了新的数据、任务和方法基础。

Abstract: Deep learning models have been increasingly applied to Time Series Forecasting (TSF) in recent years. Transformer-based and MLP-based models have both been used effectively on many real-world TSF regression benchmarks, and there is ongoing debate as to which family of methods is best. While these benchmarks have drawn much attention, it is also worth noting that many current datasets and methods assume approximate periodicity in the time series. In this work, we focus on a new TSF task without periodicity: anticipating falls during humanoid locomotion, on the basis of egocentric vision and proprioception. When the locomotion trajectories are sufficiently diverse, periodicity is violated. We contribute two new benchmark datasets (one from simulation, one from real hardware), showing that periodicity is violated and recent deep TSF methods struggle on these benchmarks. We also propose a novel deep learning architecture that exploits both endogenous and exogenous variables and a training process that rigorously enforces i.i.d sampling of training examples. Our results show statistically significant improvement over prior art in multiple experimental conditions, by 12.73% or more on the real data and 10.40% or more on the simulation data. Code and datasets will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [34] [Conditional Timed Partial Orders: An Expressive and Interpretable Framework for Robot Task Specification and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07905)
*Sebastian Escobar,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出条件定时偏序（cTPO）框架，在定时偏序（TPO）基础上引入更丰富的相对时间约束和基于环境条件的事件激活，并通过分解算法提升规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TPO只能表达偏序事件和简单时间约束，难以描述具有条件分支及复杂相对时序的机器人任务；直接将cTPO规划转化为MILP会产生规模过大的模型，导致计算不可 tractable。

Method: 将cTPO规划建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），并设计分解算法，将一个复杂cTPO划分为多个较小的子TPO，依次求解对应的MILP问题。理论上证明该分解具有完备性，并保持原规划问题的最优性。

Result: 实验表明，cTPO能够有效表达复杂机器人任务；分解方法相比单一整体MILP求解具有显著效率优势，最高可实现四个数量级的加速，同时改善任务规范的可解释性。

Conclusion: cTPO扩展了TPO的表达能力，可统一描述复杂时间关系与条件事件激活。所提出的保持最优性的分解算法缓解了大规模MILP的计算瓶颈，为复杂机器人任务规划提供了高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Timed Partial Orders (TPOs), originally proposed for workflows, provide an interpretable framework for robot task specification with planning algorithms based on mixed-integer linear programming (MILP). However, TPOs are limited in expressivity, capturing only partial-order events with simple timing constraints. In this paper, we introduce Conditional TPOs (cTPOs), which extend TPOs with richer relative-timing constraints and conditional event activations based on environmental conditions. We show that planning for cTPOs also reduces to an MILP problem; however, the added expressivity results in significantly larger MILPs that can become computationally intractable. To address this challenge, we propose a decomposition algorithm that partitions a cTPO into smaller sub-TPOs, yielding a sequence of smaller MILP problems. We prove that this decomposition is complete and preserves plan optimality while improving the interpretability of complex tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of cTPOs as a task specification framework and the efficiency of our decomposition approach, achieving up to four orders of magnitude speedup over the monolithic MILP.

</details>


### [35] [M3-Tele: A Unified Multimodal Teleoperational Framework for Compliant Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07859)
*Hengxiang Chen,Shenwen Deng,Yujian Ma,Gan Ma,Qiang Li,Nutan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-Tele统一多模态遥操作框架，用于实现具备柔顺控制能力的全身移动操作，并同步采集视觉、触觉、力觉和本体感知数据。实验表明，该框架显著提升了接触丰富任务中的遥操作性能及数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作与数据采集框架通常未能同时考虑多模态感知和全身协调操作，导致接触丰富任务中的交互稳定性、示范采集效率与数据质量不足，进而限制下游策略学习效果。

Method: 构建M3-Tele统一框架，将视觉、触觉、力觉和本体感知进行对齐采集，并结合全身协调控制与柔顺调节，实现移动操作过程中的稳定物理交互。通过控制器进行力跟踪和接触管理，并利用用户研究及Diffusion Policy验证系统性能和多模态感知的作用。

Result: 控制器将力跟踪误差从4.132 N降至0.346 N，将每次试验的接触丢失次数从2.46降至0.02次，触觉形变误差降低65%。在四项移动操作任务的用户研究中，系统表现出较好的可靠性和可用性。Diffusion Policy实验进一步表明，联合使用触觉与力觉信息能够提升策略学习效果。

Conclusion: M3-Tele有效统一了多模态感知、全身协调和柔顺控制，能够提升接触丰富任务中的遥操作稳定性、示范数据质量及下游策略学习效果，验证了触觉与力觉协同感知的重要价值。

Abstract: Executing contact-rich tasks efficiently requires the seamless integration of whole-body coordination and physical compliance regulation. However, existing teleoperation and data-collection frameworks often overlook the joint consideration of multimodal perception and coordinated whole-body operation. This limitation can reduce the efficiency and quality of demonstration collection, thereby affecting the effectiveness of downstream policy learning. In this work, we present \textbf{M3-Tele}: A Unified \underline{M}ultimodal \underline{Tele}operational Framework for Compliant Whole-Body \underline{M}obile \underline{M}anipulation, enabling stable physical interaction and capturing aligned visual, tactile, force, and proprioceptive observations during task execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves contact-rich teleoperation performance. The proposed controller reduces the force tracking error from 4.132~N to 0.346~N, the contact loss from 2.46 to 0.02 events per trial and the tactile deformation error by 65\%. User studies across four mobile manipulation tasks also verify the reliability and usability of the proposed system. Furthermore, Diffusion Policy experiments highlight the value of joint tactile and force sensing.

</details>


### [36] [ComVLA: Communication-Aware Split Inference for VLA Models in 6G-Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07838)
*Boliang Liu,Wint Yi Poe,Jingyun Di,Riccardo Trivisonno,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ComVLA利用语言指令引导视觉令牌选择，并根据无线信道容量动态调整VLA输入规模，在显著降低通信与推理开销的同时，仅造成小幅任务成功率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向6G联网机器人，云端运行大型视觉-语言-动作模型需要传输大量感知数据，而无线链路容量受限。现有语义通信方法通常依赖信道特定训练，视觉令牌剪枝方法又未考虑信道约束，因此需要联合优化VLA推理与无线通信。

Method: 提出ComVLA框架，利用语言指令中包含的密集语义信息判断重要视觉令牌，并结合当前信道容量自适应分配视觉令牌预算。该方法将每步传输的视觉令牌数量从512压缩至32，同时适配Rayleigh和Rician衰落信道。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，相较原始OpenVLA-OFT，ComVLA使推理计算量降低74%、推理时延降低22%；平均任务成功率为95.4%，较基线96.9%下降1.5个百分点，并能够满足两类衰落信道下的容量预算。

Conclusion: 语言引导的视觉令牌压缩能够有效协调通信约束与VLA推理需求，在保持较高任务性能的同时显著降低计算、时延和传输开销，表明VLA模型与无线通信的协同设计适用于联网机器人场景。

Abstract: Connected robotics is an emerging 6G application where mobile robots follow natural-language instructions to manipulate physical objects. The Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models that enable this are too large to run on the robot; a common trend is to offload inference to the cloud. The wireless link, however, limits how much sensing data the edge can transmit per control step. Two recent lines address this constraint: semantic communication codecs compress sensor data but require channel-specific retraining, and VLA token pruners select tokens from image but ignore the channel. Our insight is that the dense semantic information contained in the language already indicates which visual tokens matter. We propose ComVLA, a framework that uses this language guidance to adapt the VLA token budget to the channel capacity. Transmitting 32 tokens instead of 512 on the LIBERO benchmark, ComVLA cuts inference compute by 74% and inference latency by 22% versus the original OpenVLA-OFT baseline, at a cost of 1.5 pp in average task success (95.4% vs. 96.9%), and it stays within the capacity budget under Rayleigh and Rician fading. These results demonstrate that co-designing VLA inference and wireless communication is a practical direction for 6G-connected robotics.

</details>


### [37] [Dex-X: Learning Visual-Tactile Dexterous Manipulation From Human Videos with Simulated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07747)
*Ruoqu Chen,Feixiang Ruan,Liu Cao,Zihao Wang,Botian Xu,Shiqin Tong,Jiajun Liu,Mingzhi Pei,Chenyu Zhang,Wanli Xing,Kaifeng Zhang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEX-X通过仿真将单目人类视频重建为手物交互，并利用物理接触动力学生成缺失的触觉监督，从而训练可部署的视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略，实现从人类视频到真实机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频包含丰富的灵巧操作行为，但通常缺乏机器人接触交互所需的触觉信息。传统方法依赖大量机器人侧数据采集，因此论文探讨能否仅利用人类视频和仿真学习可部署的视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略。

Method: DEX-X首先根据单目人类演示在仿真环境中重建手-物交互，将仿真中的物理接触动力学作为触觉信息补全机制。随后利用恢复的视觉与触觉信息训练灵巧操作教师策略，并将其蒸馏为使用点云观测和触觉传感器的可部署策略，最后进行仿真到真实机器人的零样本迁移。

Result: 教师策略在仿真中六类任务上的平均成功率为65.9%。蒸馏后的视觉-触觉策略在真实立方体抓取任务上达到93%的成功率，在更具挑战性的桌面清洁任务上达到53%。在物体抓取任务中，策略还展现出对未见物体几何形状的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 仿真交互能够有效弥补人类视频中缺失的物理接触监督，是连接互联网规模人类视频数据与可部署灵巧机器人技能的重要桥梁。该方法降低了机器人侧数据采集需求，但其性能仍受交互重建质量、仿真与现实差异以及复杂接触任务难度的影响。

Abstract: Human videos are an abundant source of dexterous manipulation behaviors, but they lack tactile information that is crucial for contact-rich interaction. This raises a fundamental question: can robots learn deployable visual-tactile dexterous manipulation policies from human video demonstrations without robot-side data collection? We present DEX-X, a framework for learning visual-tactile dexterous manipulation from human videos through simulation. Our key insight is that simulation can serve as a tactile completion engine. Given monocular human demonstrations, DEX-X reconstructs hand-object interactions in simulation, where physically grounded contact dynamics provide tactile supervision unavailable in the original videos. Leveraging this recovered tactile information, we train visual-tactile dexterous manipulation policies and distill them into deployable policies operating on point-cloud observations and tactile sensing. We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a dexterous hand-arm platform across diverse grasping and contact-rich tool-use tasks. The teacher policy achieves 65.9% average success across six task categories in simulation, while the distilled visual-tactile policy achieves 93% success on real-world cube picking and 53% on the challenging table-cleaning task. Zero-shot generalization to unseen object geometries is also observed on object-picking tasks. Our results suggest that simulated interaction is a key bridge between human videos and deployable dexterous manipulation policies, providing the missing physical supervision needed for scalable robot skill learning from Internet-scale human video data.

</details>


### [38] [ICI-VLA: In-Context Imitation with Spatiotemporally Aligned Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07581)
*Songhua Yang,Ziyu Liu,Xuetao Li,Ruqi Xiao,Kangxin Zhu,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ICI-VLA提出一种无需测试时梯度更新的视觉-语言-动作策略适配框架，通过检索与当前子任务在语义、时间和几何上对齐的微型示范，实现少样本测试时适应，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA策略适应新操作环境通常依赖额外梯度更新，需要任务数据和计算资源，难以快速部署。现有方法还常采用动作专用的多模态融合结构，限制了原生文本生成接口的利用。ICI-VLA旨在保持策略参数固定和文本生成接口不变的前提下，通过上下文示范提升跨任务适应能力。

Method: 该框架在离线阶段完成全部参数更新，推理阶段仅检索并输入微型示范。它将长轨迹切分为带语义标签的短示例，并训练检索编码器RD-Encoder；利用动态时间规整DTW挖掘正样本，使检索上下文在任务阶段和空间几何上与当前子任务对齐。此外，Target Action Masking通过遮蔽或扰动上下文中的目标动作，降低策略直接复制示范动作的倾向，促使其更多依赖当前观测生成动作。

Result: ICI-VLA在LIBERO上的平均成功率为97.7%，在RoboTwin 2.0上为60.4%，较该基准最高报告平均性能提升19.3个百分点；在四项真实物理任务中达到83.2%的平均成功率。结果表明，时空对齐的检索示范能够有效增强固定VLA策略。

Conclusion: 研究证明，VLA策略无需测试时参数更新，也可以通过检索相关示范实现有效的少样本适应。DTW驱动的时空对齐和Target Action Masking是提升检索有效性、避免动作机械复制的关键设计，但其性能仍依赖示范库质量、检索准确性以及不同任务间的可迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are commonly adapted to new manipulation settings through additional gradient updates, which limits rapid deployment when task-specific data or compute is scarce. We present ICI-VLA, a training and retrieval framework that equips a text-action VLM with few-shot test-time adaptation through in-context demonstrations. Unlike mainstream VLA designs based on action-specific multimodal fusion, ICI-VLA retains the native text-generation interface. ICI-VLA updates its parameters only during offline training; at inference, the policy remains fixed and conditions action generation on retrieved micro-demonstrations. The framework decomposes long trajectories into short, semantically labeled examples and trains an RD-Encoder with positives mined by Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), aligning the retrieved context with the phase and geometry of the current subtask. We further introduce Target Action Masking, a context-corruption objective designed to reduce direct action copying and increase reliance on the current observation. ICI-VLA reaches average success rates of 97.7% on LIBERO and 60.4% on RoboTwin 2.0, exceeding the highest reported baseline average on RoboTwin 2.0 by 19.3 percentage points. It also achieves 83.2% across four physical tasks. These results indicate that a fixed VLA policy can benefit from conditioning on spatiotemporally aligned demonstrations at test time.

</details>

*Kazuki Sugihara,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向飞行人形机器人的实时全身模型预测控制（MPC）框架，实现天花板等反重力环境中的双足行走。核心方法是以推力变化率作为控制输入，并对接触力施加平滑约束，以避免接触切换引发的推力突变和控制不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有飞行人形机器人的运动能力主要限于空中飞行和地面行走，难以适应复杂三维环境。在天花板等反重力场景中，接触状态切换会导致动力学结构不连续，进而产生推力尖峰和控制不稳定，因此需要一种兼顾实时性与运动连续性的全身控制方法。

Method: 首先，将推力变化率而非推力本身作为全身MPC的控制输入，使推力轨迹在接触切换时保持连续，同时保留最优控制问题的稀疏结构以支持快速求解。其次，针对反重力环境中缺乏自然支撑力的问题，对足部接触力沿法向的分量设置下界，并在双支撑阶段进行平滑转移。最后，通过仿真和硬件实验验证所提出的实时控制框架。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验均实现了飞行人形机器人在非地面环境中的反重力双足行走。实验表明，该方法能够抑制接触切换过程中的推力突变，并保持全身运动与接触力控制的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次展示了可变形飞行机器人上的多接触全身MPC，以及飞行人形机器人在地面之外环境中的行走能力。以推力变化率为输入并结合接触力下界平滑转移，为复杂三维环境中的连续、稳定行走提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Flying humanoids are expected to perform tasks in diverse environments, while their existing locomotion is mainly limited to aerial flight and ground walking. The capability to move in complex three-dimensional space can greatly expand their application range. For such walking motion on ceilings and similar anti-gravity environments, whole-body MPC is effective. However, the discontinuous changes in dynamic structure accompanying contact switching during walking can induce thrust spikes, resulting in control instability. Therefore, in this work, we propose and implement a real-time whole-body MPC framework for anti-gravity bipedal walking. First, we formulate whole-body MPC using the time derivative of thrust, namely thrust-rate, as the control input. This formulation guarantees continuity of the thrust trajectory during contact switching while preserving the sparse structure of the optimal control problem for fast computation. Second, we address the lack of natural support forces in anti-gravity environments. We introduce lower bounds on the foot-normal component of the contact force, and smoothly transfer them during the doublesupport phase. Finally, we implement the proposed framework and demonstrate anti-gravity walking by a flying humanoid through simulation and a hardware experiment. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of multi-contact whole-body MPC for a transformable aerial robot and walking by a flying humanoid beyond the ground.

</details>


### [40] [PhysReal: Learning Real-World Deformable Object Physics via Hybrid Constitutive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07532)
*Yinan Deng,Jianqiao Song,Yisi Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Jiahui Wang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysReal是一种基于视频观测的可变形物体物理学习与仿真框架，通过混合专家模型与神经本构模型、可微MPM仿真器及3DGS渲染器，实现对空间异质材料动力学的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实可变形物体的动力学通常源于复杂且空间不均匀的材料响应，传统固定形式的物理模型难以准确描述，而仅依赖神经网络又缺乏可解释性与物理合理性。因此，需要一种能够从稀疏视觉观测中恢复并预测物体动力学的统一方法。

Method: PhysReal将具有物理可解释性的解析专家本构模型与神经本构残差结合，并通过可微物质点法（MPM）模拟器和3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）渲染器进行视觉驱动的物理学习。模型使用空间分布式局部块表示连续变化的本构场，以刻画材料的局部差异；同时采用渐进式课程优化策略，依次学习全局材料属性、空间变化的局部参数和神经本构残差，并结合运动监督与掩码监督。

Result: 在多种可变形物体交互场景上的实验表明，PhysReal在动态重建和未来状态预测方面优于对比方法，并能够有效处理具有空间异质材料响应的对象。

Conclusion: 通过融合解析物理先验、神经残差建模、空间变化参数化和可微仿真，PhysReal提升了从视觉观测学习真实可变形物体动力学的准确性与可解释性，并展现出支持后续机器人应用的潜力。

Abstract: Learning physically plausible dynamics from visual observations is essential for interactive world models and embodied agents. However, modeling real-world deformable objects remains challenging because their dynamics often arise from complex, spatially heterogeneous material responses. To address this challenge, we propose PhysReal, a video-driven framework for learning and simulating the underlying physics of real deformable objects. PhysReal integrates a spatially varying hybrid expert-neural constitutive model with a differentiable MPM simulator and 3DGS renderer. Analytical expert models provide interpretable physical priors, while neural constitutive residuals capture material responses beyond predefined formulations. Spatially distributed patches parameterize the constitutive field, enabling a continuous representation of local material variations. To organize the identification of this model from sparse visual observations, we adopt a progressive curriculum that sequentially optimizes global material properties, spatially varying local parameters, and neural constitutive residuals, together with complementary motion and mask supervision. Extensive experiments on diverse deformable-object interactions demonstrate that PhysReal achieves superior performance in dynamic reconstruction and future-state prediction, while showing strong potential for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [41] [P$^2$Calib: Utilizing Pattern Priors for LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07516)
*Xiangcheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出P^2Calib，一种利用标定板几何先验改进LiDAR-相机外参标定的方法，针对四孔标定流程中的孔中心提取误差提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四孔标定方法的精度主要受LiDAR侧孔中心估计限制，稀疏角度覆盖会导致拟合不稳定，混合像素还会引入残余误差，因此需要利用标定板的已知结构约束提高鲁棒性。

Method: 首先将孔的已知半径作为拟合约束，以缓解稀疏角度覆盖造成的孔中心偏移；随后利用四孔构成的刚性矩形布局建立全局一致性约束，校正各孔之间的残余误差。两类几何先验被集成到交互式外参标定工具中，形成完整标定流程。

Result: 在模拟数据和真实数据上，相比基线方法，P^2Calib分别将联合配准残差降低90%和82%，将留出重投影误差降低96%和77%，表明其在不同数据条件下均能显著提升标定精度。

Conclusion: 通过结合孔半径和四孔矩形布局等CAD几何先验，P^2Calib有效改善了LiDAR孔中心提取质量，并显著提高了LiDAR-相机外参标定的准确性与可靠性。

Abstract: Target-based LiDAR-camera extrinsic calibration is a prerequisite for multi-sensor fusion in robotics. However, in the widely adopted four-hole pipeline, calibration accuracy is bottlenecked by LiDAR-side hole-center extraction, which suffers from sparse angular coverage and mixed-pixel corruption. This paper presents P$^2$Calib, which exploits pattern priors, geometric constraints specified by the CAD model of the target board, to improve calibration accuracy. First, we incorporate the known hole radius as a fitting constraint to prevent center estimates from degrading under sparse angular coverage. Building on the improved hole estimates, we further enforce the rigid rectangular layout of the four holes as a global consistency constraint to correct residual errors across holes. Both priors are integrated into an interactive calibration tool that provides a complete extrinsic calibration pipeline. Experiments on simulated and real datasets show that P$^2$Calib lowers the joint registration residual by 90\% and 82\% and the held-out reprojection error by 96\% and 77\% over the baseline. Code, this https URL , and data will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [42] [Functional-SLAM: Interaction-Aware Mapping with Online Functional Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07497)
*Xinggang Hu,Chenyangguang Zhang,Zihan Zhu,Ruida Zhang,Xiangkui Zhang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Functional-SLAM将功能性三维场景图作为在线SLAM状态持续维护，实现实时构建功能地图，并利用功能拓扑辅助回环检测与位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM系统主要建模几何结构，缺乏支持精细机器人交互所需的功能关系；同时，已有功能场景图方法依赖离线重建，难以满足真实环境探索中的实时交互需求。

Method: 框架以锚点关键帧几何和功能上下文约束为基础，持续递归维护场景图节点；通过时间关系融合多帧证据，确认稳定的功能边；在视觉外观重复或纹理退化的场景中，引入功能拓扑补充视觉回环候选，以增强回环检测和位姿优化。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够在线高效构建稳定的功能地图，相较离线方法显著降低运行时间，同时保持具有竞争力的精度。与其他SLAM系统相比，功能拓扑辅助的回环检测进一步提升了位姿估计准确性。

Conclusion: Functional-SLAM验证了将功能场景图纳入在线SLAM状态的可行性，为实时机器人交互提供了统一的几何与功能建模框架，并在效率、地图稳定性和定位精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Existing SLAM systems lack modeling of the functional relations required for fine-grained robotic interaction. Functional 3D scene graphs can represent relations between objects and interaction elements, but existing methods rely on offline reconstruction, making them inadequate for real-time interaction in real-world exploration. To address this limitation, we propose Functional-SLAM, the first framework that continuously and recursively maintains a functional scene graph as an online SLAM state. The framework combines anchor-keyframe geometry with functional-context constraints for persistent node maintenance, accumulates multi-frame evidence through temporal relations to commit stable functional edges, and supplements visual loop-closure candidates with functional topology in scenes with repetitive appearance or degraded texture. Experiments show that Functional-SLAM efficiently constructs stable functional maps online, substantially improving runtime over offline methods while maintaining highly competitive accuracy. Compared with peer SLAM systems, it further improves pose estimation accuracy through functional-topology-assisted loop closure. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [43] [Wearable Multimodal Human-Machine Interface for Integrated Hand Intentions Decoding in Dynamic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07495)
*Jiaxuan Li,Yinshi Wu,Xiao Zhang,Hongyu Wang,Renzhen Le,Zhenzhi Ying,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可穿戴多意图解码人机接口（MI-DHMI），融合高通量表面肌电（sEMG）传感器与手部、前臂惯性测量单元（IMU），用于同步解码手势、抓握力和手部姿态。系统在非受限腕部和前臂运动下仍具备较高准确性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作抓握接口通常难以同时满足多种手部意图解码的丰富性、精确性与可穿戴性，且上肢自由运动会引入sEMG变化和解码误差。因此，需要一种兼具轻便佩戴、鲁棒感知和多意图同步解码能力的接口。

Method: 构建融合sEMG与手部/前臂IMU的MI-DHMI硬件系统，并设计统一的多意图同步解码框架。通过多模态深度学习选择与手指运动真正相关的sEMG成分，同时结合低噪声硬件设计，降低非受限上肢运动导致的信号干扰。研究还采用消融实验和在线任务实验评估系统性能。

Result: 在非受限腕部和前臂运动条件下，手势识别准确率超过97%，抓握力估计达到R²=0.95，手部姿态解码结果与实际姿态一致，并优于基线设备和算法。消融实验验证了解码框架各组成部分的有效性；倒液体和物体抓取在线实验进一步证明了系统在高稳定性遥操作任务中的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了一种全可穿戴、多意图同步解码的人机接口，能够较鲁棒地识别手势、估计抓握力并解码手部姿态。其综合性能和可穿戴特性为普适性遥操作及高质量人机交互提供了有效技术方案。

Abstract: Under ubiquitous teleoperation environments with optically challenging conditions, an interface for tele-operated grasping that combines wearability with precise decoding of hand intentions (hand pose, gestures, and grasping force) is essential. Yet, existing interfaces often fall short in meeting these demands, compromising either the diversity of multiple intentions decoding or wearability. To address this, we developed a novel Multiple Intentions Decoding Human-Machine Interface (MI-DHMI) that integrates high-throughput surface electromyography (sEMG) sensors with hand-mounted and forearm-mounted inertial measurement units (IMUs). The developed interface is supported by a unified framework for simultaneous multiple intentions decoding. By employing multimodal deep learning and hardware design with a low noise floor, the decoding framework selectively focuses on the sEMG components that are genuinely associated with finger movements. This effectively reduces decoding errors caused by sEMG variability during unconstrained upper-limb motions, thereby significantly enhancing robustness. Even under unconstrained wrist and forearm motion, the interface achieves a gesture recognition accuracy exceeding 97%, grasping force estimation with $R^2 = 0.95$, and hand pose decoding consistent with the actual hand pose, outperforming baseline devices and algorithms. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed decoding framework. Finally, two online experiments were conducted to validate the device, demonstrating its superior performance in high-stability tasks, including a pouring task and object grasping. The developed interface provides a new solution of a fully wearable, multiple intentions decoding system, offering effective support for ubiquitous teleoperation and contributing to the advancement of human-machine interaction research.

</details>


### [44] [SMaRT-Tug: Structured Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Based Tugboat-Barge Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07445)
*Junkai Lu,Jiadong Zhao,Jiacheng Zhang,Wenqi Zhao,Hao Gen Chia,Qun Shen Png,Germaine Ee,Chengyang He,Yifeng Zhang,Nathanael Tan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自主拖船协同操纵的物理仿真与多智能体强化学习框架，通过GPU加速海洋动力学模拟和带结构化控制先验的去中心化MAPPO，实现多拖船对大型船舶的协同推移，并在多种操纵任务中表现出较好的可靠性、精度与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口物流和船舶操纵需要多艘拖船协同作业，但协同推移受到耦合流体动力学、低阻力、环境扰动、欠驱动船体以及复杂接触交互的共同影响。传统控制方法依赖简化模型和固定编队，适应性有限；学习方法则受制于缺乏可扩展且具有物理真实性的训练环境。

Method: 构建物理驱动、GPU加速的多智能体海洋仿真器，集成定制浮力模型、波浪模型和流体动力阻力模型，以支持大规模训练。在此基础上，训练去中心化MAPPO策略，并引入结构化控制先验，以提升训练稳定性并约束拖船维持可行的协同推移构型。实验覆盖直线航行、转向和减速任务，并与PID控制器及集中式PPO基线进行比较。

Result: 去中心化MAPPO结合结构化控制先验后，在直线航行、转向和减速任务中表现出比PID控制器和集中式PPO更可靠、更准确的操纵性能。该方法还能够零样本适应更复杂的海况和高级操纵任务，并在仅使用两艘拖船训练的情况下，零样本扩展至三艘和四艘拖船协同作业。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将高真实性海洋物理仿真、结构化控制先验与去中心化多智能体强化学习结合，有望提高协同拖船操纵的稳定性、可行性和跨场景泛化能力。其主要贡献在于构建了可扩展的训练基础设施，并验证了策略在海况变化和团队规模扩展下的零样本迁移潜力；但摘要未说明实船或硬件在环验证、计算开销及不同动力学参数下的定量性能边界。

Abstract: Autonomous tugboating is central for automating maritime operations such as port logistics and vessel maneuvering, where multiple tugboats must cooperatively transport/manipulate a larger vessel. Collaborative pushing in this setting is challenging due to coupled hydrodynamics, low resistance, strong environmental disturbances, underactuated barge dynamics, and contact-rich interactions. Conventional control methods often rely on simplified models and fixed configurations, which limit their adaptability, while learning-based approaches are constrained by the lack of scalable and physically realistic training environments. We address these challenges by introducing a physics-based, GPU-accelerated simulation and learning framework for collaborative tugboat manipulation. Our simulator incorporates a customized buoyancy model, wave modeling, and hydrodynamic resistance, and supports large-scale multi-agent training under marine dynamics. In this simulator, we train a decentralized MAPPO (Multi-Agent PPO) policy augmented with a structured control prior (SCP) to improve training stability and maintain feasible pushing configurations. We evaluate our learned policy on straight-line transit, turning, and deceleration tasks, where we show that our decentralized framework yields more reliable and accurate maneuvering performance compared to a PID-based controller and a centralized PPO baseline. We further demonstrate zero-shot generalization to more challenging sea states and advanced maneuvers, as well as zero-shot scalability to larger teams of three and four tugboats despite training with only two agents.

</details>


### [45] [Open-Set Ego-Noise Separation for Legged-Robot Audition via Annotation-Free Adaptation and Pretrained-Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07440)
*Koki Shoda,Jun Younes Louhi Kasahara,Aoba Koyanagi,Qi An,Atsushi Yamashita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向腿式机器人听觉的开放集自我噪声分离框架，通过无标注适配与预训练模型迁移，在不预先限定环境声音类别的情况下去除机器人自身噪声并保留环境声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人行走产生的脚步冲击、关节回差振动和电机噪声会严重污染麦克风录音，削弱环境感知能力。现有方法通常依赖人工标注或单独采集的纯自我噪声数据，难以适应不同机器人及开放环境。

Method: 首先利用RecurGraph对无标注录音进行片段筛选：将音频片段嵌入聚合为中心表示，并在音频嵌入图上进行分数传播，以选出自我噪声占主导的片段。随后，将这些片段与大规模声音事件数据集中的多样环境声音混合，构造混合音频—目标音频监督对。最后，通过Transfer-DiT将通用零样本神经分离器迁移到目标机器人，实现开放集自我噪声分离。

Result: 在双足和四足机器人上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠筛选自我噪声片段，并在分离质量及下游任务性能方面优于基线分离器。

Conclusion: 研究证明，无需纯自我噪声录音或人工片段级标注，便可通过无标注适配和预训练模型迁移实现高质量的开放集机器人自我噪声分离，具有较好的跨机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes an open-set ego-noise separation framework for legged-robot audition via annotation-free adaptation and pretrained-model transfer. The framework removes robot-specific ego-noise while preserving environmental sounds whose classes are not specified in advance. Acoustic sensing provides cues about a robot's surroundings beyond the visual field, but walking-induced ego-noise from footstep impacts, joint-backlash rattling, and motor noise severely contaminates the recordings. The framework first uses RecurGraph to select ego-noise-dominant clips from the unlabeled recordings by aggregating clip embeddings into an embedding centroid and propagating scores over an audio-embedding graph. The selected clips are mixed with diverse environmental sounds from a large-scale sound-event dataset to provide paired mixture--target supervision for open-set separation. Transfer-DiT then adapts a general-purpose zero-shot neural separator to achieve high-fidelity open-set ego-noise separation for the target robot. Experiments with bipedal and quadrupedal robots show reliable clip selection and improvements in separation quality and downstream task performance over baseline separators. These results demonstrate the feasibility of annotation-free adaptation without separately recorded ego-noise-only data or manual clip-level annotations.

</details>


### [46] [CALM: Configuration-Aware Human Intervention Boundaries During Robot Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07430)
*Xinting Gao,Sipu Zhu,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，机器人手臂构型会显著影响人类接近时的干预距离；完全前伸的手臂使参与者更早停止，而空间尺度主要影响舒适度和瞳孔反应。研究提出CALM模型，将停止距离分布转化为与构型相关的人群覆盖边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少系统考察机器人身体构型如何影响人类接近行为及干预风险。研究旨在区分物理安全距离、行为干预概率与主观舒适度，并为机器人接近规划提供构型敏感的决策依据。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计，招募41名参与者，比较4种类人机器人手臂构型和2种空间尺度。测量最终停止距离、主观舒适度及探索性眼动指标，并提出Configuration-Aware Limit Model（CALM），将停止距离分布转换为不同构型下的人群覆盖边界；进一步通过一维规划示例评估构型重配置的作用。

Result: 手臂完全前伸相较于手臂下垂，使参与者的最终停止距离增加约31—36厘米。空间尺度主要影响舒适度和瞳孔反应，但未显著改变停止距离。CALM估计的80%覆盖边界为0.88—1.47米。在相同的名义20%干预概率约束下，重配置可实现1.10米的接近目标，而保持手臂完全前伸时无法实现。

Conclusion: 机器人身体构型应被纳入接近行为与运动规划的决策变量，而不能仅视为固定外形参数。规划系统应同时建模物理安全、人的行为干预概率和主观代价；CALM为依据实验分布设置构型相关边界提供了可行方法。

Abstract: How robot body configuration shapes human intervention during approach remains underexplored. We conducted a within-participants study with 41 participants, measuring final stopping distance, subjective comfort, and exploratory eye-tracking responses across four humanoid arm configurations and two spatial scales. Full forward arm extension increased stopping distance by approximately 31-36 cm relative to arms-down. Spatial scale primarily affected comfort and pupil responses without a detectable stopping-distance shift. We introduce the Configuration-Aware Limit Model (CALM), which translates stopping-distance distributions into configuration-dependent population-coverage boundaries. Estimated boundaries at 80% coverage ranged from 0.88 to 1.47 m. In an illustrative one-dimensional planning analysis, reconfiguration enabled a 1.10 m approach goal that was unreachable with arms remaining fully extended under the same nominal pointwise 20% intervention-probability constraint. These findings support treating body configuration as a planning variable while distinguishing physical safety, behavioral intervention, and subjective cost.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenWAM，一个将世界—动作模型（WAM）预训练拆分为可组合模块的开放研究框架，并通过受控实验总结设计原则，进一步构建在约6400小时人类第一视角与机器人数据上预训练的OpenWAM-α，在仿真和真实机器人任务中取得稳定的顶级表现。

Result: 实验表明：上游知识迁移依赖能力足够强的生成式骨干和紧凑且信息丰富的潜在空间；世界—动作协同需要专门的动作建模容量、明确的世界到动作信息流，以及同步的联合去噪；具身预训练主要提升域外泛化能力。采用自我中心数据与机器人数据的一阶段协同训练，可同时整合世界覆盖范围与动作落地能力。OpenWAM-α在八项仿真基准和真实机器人实验中，覆盖单臂、双臂及灵巧手等多种形态，均取得稳定且具有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 本文将WAM预训练从紧耦合的单体系统转化为可拆解、可复现和可扩展的实验体系，并提炼出影响性能的关键设计原则。OpenWAM-α验证了这些原则在仿真到现实迁移中的有效性；开放基础设施、评估协议、预训练模型和数据配方有望推动后续世界—动作模型研究。

Abstract: World-Action Models inherit world knowledge from video-generative priors, and channel it into executable control signals through embodied experience. Existing systems, however, are monolithic: the generative backbone, visual representation, architecture, information flow, inference procedure, and training data are tightly coupled, obscuring which design choices matter and why. We introduce OpenWAM, an open research stack that turns world-action pretraining into a controlled experimental program. OpenWAM-Infra factorizes the WAM design space into composable modules with unified training, inference, deployment, and evaluation. On this substrate, OpenWAM-Study examines three questions through controlled experiments: what to inherit, how world and action learning interact, and how their synergy scales; and distills three principles: upstream knowledge transfers through a sufficiently capable generative backbone and a compact, information-rich latent space; world-action synergy requires dedicated action capacity, explicit world-to-action information flow, and synchronized joint denoising; and embodied pretraining principally improves out-of-domain generalization, with one-stage co-training over egocentric and robot data integrating world coverage and action grounding. Composing these principles, we build OpenWAM-{\alpha}, an open WAM pretrained on roughly 6,400 hours of egocentric human and robot data and evaluated across simulation and real-world benchmarks. Across the eight simulation benchmarks and the real-robot experiments, which together span embodiments from single-arm and bimanual manipulation to dexterous hands, OpenWAM-{\alpha} delivers consistently excellent performance, sustaining its top-tier standing from simulation to the physical world. We release the full stack, including infrastructure, evaluation protocols, pretrained models, and data recipes, to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [48] [How Long Until Your Robot Ignores You? A Safety Benchmark for LLM Orchestrators in Human-Humanoid Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07288)
*Aulon Bajrami,Mohamed Elshamouty,Werner Kraus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向人形机器人协作中大语言模型编排器的安全基准，并评估不同模型与上下文管理策略对合规性和安全违规的影响。结果显示，模型家族显著决定安全下限；上下文窗口可减少一般行为问题，却可能增加特定模型的安全违规；Gemini在按规则限速而非简单拒绝方面表现出更好的比例合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人安全系统通常采用确定性的允许或拒绝机制，缺乏对大语言模型在自然语言编排场景下连续合规行为的系统评估。本文旨在衡量模型从过度合规、欠合规到完全违规的行为谱系，并验证其在人形机器人协作中的安全可靠性。

Method: 构建基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的安全基准，以ISO 10218-2:2025保护措施为基础定义五项可测试安全不变量、四级合规分类（正确合规、过度合规、欠合规、完全违规）和三级评估流程：文本提示、模拟传感器—执行器闭环、实体机器人验证。第一层测试覆盖Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash及本地qwen3:8b，在完整上下文和滑动窗口条件下进行40个、每个100轮的会话评估。

Result: Claude和Gemini的违规率接近于零，而GPT-4o-mini每个会话最多出现13次违规，表明模型家族决定安全表现下限。上下文管理使所有云端模型的平均行为问题减少42%至57%，但GPT-4o-mini的平均违规次数从3.8次升至7.2次，显示一般行为质量与安全违规可能发生分离。只有Gemini持续表现出比例合规，即将运动速度限制在规则规定的最大值以内，而非直接拒绝任务。初步模拟结果复现了模型排序及GPT-4o-mini的故障模式反转。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估大语言模型作为安全感知型机器人决策者提供了可操作框架。初步证据表明，提升上下文管理并不必然提升安全性，模型选择和对比例合规行为的支持同样关键。由于模拟和实体机器人测试尚未完成，当前结论仍属初步结果，后续需要通过多层验证确认其在真实人形机器人协作中的泛化性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed to orchestrate robot behavior through natural-language interfaces, yet no benchmark exists to evaluate their reliability as safety-aware decision makers in human-humanoid collaboration. Unlike deterministic safety systems that enforce binary allow/deny decisions, LLM-based orchestrators exhibit a compliance spectrum ranging from overcompliance (refusing safe actions) to full safety violations. This paper introduces the first safety benchmarking environment for LLM orchestrators in human-humanoid collaboration, built on a Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based architecture with safety invariants grounded in ISO 10218-2:2025 protective measures. The benchmark defines five testable safety invariants, a four-level compliance taxonomy (correct compliance, overcompliance, undercompliance, full violation), and a three-layer evaluation pipeline (text prompting, simulated sensor-actuator loops, and physical validation on a Unitree G1 EDU humanoid). We report Layer-1 results: three cloud backends (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash) and a local open-weights baseline (qwen3:8b) across 40 100-turn sessions under full-context and sliding-window budget conditions, while the simulation and physical layers remain ongoing. We find that (1) model family determines the safety floor, as Claude and Gemini remain at or near zero violations while GPT-4o-mini commits up to 13 per session, (2) context management dissociates two failure axes, reducing mean behavioral issues by 42-57% for every cloud backend while nearly doubling GPT-4o-mini's violations (3.8 to 7.2 per session), and (3) proportional compliance, clamping movement speed to the rule-specified maximum rather than refusing, emerges consistently only in Gemini; the preliminary simulation layer reproduces the model ranking and the GPT-4o-mini failure-mode inversion.

</details>

Abstract: This paper develops a singularity-free guiding vector field (SF-GVF) for path following on the special orthogonal group SO(3). First, we lift the Euclidean SF-GVF construction to SO(3), integrating the augmented-state approach with the intrinsic Lie-group geometry and obtaining a closed-form geometric guidance law whose integral curves converge to a designer-specified attitude path. The field is defined on a dense open subset of SO(3), excluding only the measure-zero antipodal set - a manifestation of the topological obstruction to continuous global stabilization on SO(3). The construction requires no per-step optimization and produces a control input intrinsically in so(3) as body angular rates. Second, we formalize the progression behavior along the path as a designer-supplied function \nu(\xi), promoting the parametric speed from an implicitly resolved degree of freedom to a first-class design specification. In contrast to the Euclidean condition v = 0, which excludes vehicles with minimum-speed constraints, the corresponding condition \omega = 0 on SO(3) is physically admissible for most platforms with active attitude control, making the progression behavior a design freedom structurally available on SO(3) but absent in the Euclidean setting. The framework's structural results are established under a bi-invariant Riemannian metric and hold uniformly across choices of path, progression, and Lyapunov gain. The framework is illustrated in simulation on self-intersecting paths under both constant and point-convergence progression behaviors.

</details>
